# Vampire Survivors Wiki (vampire.survivors.wiki) — Mecanicas, DLC, logros, historial de versiones y general
Extraido 2026-10-07. 59 paginas. Se quitaron Gallery, Update history y matrices de iconos. "| a | b |" = fila de tabla; [Nombre] = icono; "## " = encabezado. Los otros temas estan en otros archivos.

=====
# Achievements
Unlocks
Unlocks menu, with all base game achievements unlocked.
Achievements, displayed as Unlocks in-game, unlock new items, characters, stages, gameplay features, or gold coins when completed. When they are completed, the corresponding achievement will also be unlocked on the gaming platform if it supports an achievement system. For the Steam version in particular, the achievements from the base game, as well as the Legacy of the Moonspell and Tides of the Foscari DLCs, are required to achieve 100% completion. This makes a total number of 283 required achievements.
While collab DLCs have their own unlocks, due to licensing deals that can expire and stop the distribution of collaboration DLCs, the achievements may be unobtainable in the future for some people. This concern prevented the development team to implement achievement support for these DLCs on any platforms.
With all DLCs installed as of Version 1.16, the total number of achievements is 489.
- ↑ Except Version 1.15 unlocks and Version 1.16 unlocks as of summer 2026

## Trivia
- In Adventures, the "Unlocks" button is labelled as the "Progress" button instead.
=====
# Adept
Adept is a character-based mechanic in Vampire Survivors, similar to Flight. Characters who are Adept with a weapon will multiply the Cooldown of the weapon itself by 0.5, leading to that weapon firing twice as fast as it normally would. It does not multiply the player stat  Cooldown, but does stack with it.
Characters who are Adept with certain weapons include:
-  Spiritoso [DLC] and  Spiritosa [DLC], who are adept with  Mille Bolle Blu [DLC],  Boo Roo Boolle [DLC], all forms of  Penshin Fatcha and  Miracle of Multiplication.
-  Baal'Thasar [DLC] and  Baal'Thamut [DLC], who are adept with  Hell damage weapons.
-  Sargon [DLC] and  Megalo Sargon [DLC], who are adept with  FireFall,  FireBall,  FireWall and  FireStall.
-  Nameless Saint [DLC], who is adept with  Cross,  Heaven Sword,  Laurel,  108 Bocce,  108 Responsive Prayers, and  Road to Heaven.
-  Hugh Baldwin [DLC], who is adept with  Valmanway [DLC],  Million Cut [DLC],  Tyrfing [DLC], and  Rune Sword [DLC].
-  Morris Baldwin [DLC], who is adept with every weapon in the  Coat of Arms [DLC], including their evolutions, but excluding  Fuwalafuwaloo,  Legacy of Death: Soul River [DLC],  Ninth Circle [DLC], and  Vjaya Sisters [DLC].
-  Astarte [DLC], who is adept with  Star Flail [DLC].
-  Witch Actrise [DLC], who is adept with  Runetracer,  Magi-Stone,  Kyra-Stones, and  Gemma Torpor [DLC].
-  Slogra and Gaibon [DLC], who are adept with  Trident [DLC] and  Fire Wand, respectively, but not  Gungnir-Souris [DLC] and  Hellfire.
-  Zephyr [DLC], who is adept with  Knife,  Curved Knife [DLC],  Pocket Knife [DLC], their evolutions, and  Million Cut [DLC].
=====
# Ante Chamber
This article is about the DLC. For other uses, see Ante Chamber (disambiguation).
| Ante Chamber
| 
| Expansion
| Release date | 28 October 2025 (Update)
| Platform(s) | Steam, Xbox, PlayStation, Nintendo Switch, Epic Game Store, iOS and Android
| Retail price | Free
| Theme | Balatro
| {"image":"VS Ante.png","name":"Ante Chamber","theme":"Balatro","release":"28 October 2025","price":"Free","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ante Chamber out now, for free!","platform":"Steam, Xbox, PlayStation, Nintendo Switch, Epic Game Store, iOS and Android"}
They've demolished demons, battled bats, been to outer space, and failed to find any vampires, but now the Survivors face their greatest challenge yet: a bunch of Jokers. Will you fold under the pressure, or go all-in to be a Balatro-boosted bullet hell?
Official description
Ante Chamber is the seventh expansion, and second free expansion, for Vampire Survivors, released on 28 October 2025. It is themed around Balatro, a 2024 poker-based roguelike game developed by LocalThunk.
## Features
Join Jimbo and a cast of wild cards with combo potential that'll probably break the scoreboard. Get in on the action for a Full House of silly synergies, clownish combos, and bewildering Boss Blinds.
The house is full. The deck is rigged. And Luck is just another statistic. But fortune favours the bold, and if you can survive this clown car of poker puns, you can be the bullet hell even when the chips are down. 
4 New Characters (send in the clowns)
-  Jimbo: Your favourite forklift-certified clown finally has full-body representation in Vampire Survivors. Level-up bonuses from Survarot cards are boosted by x4.
-  Canio: The tragic clown in oversized finery. Has a special technique to trigger additional Survarot drafts, but won't stop frowning about it.
-  Chicot: Wears the cap and bells, but makes bosses look like the real clowns by cutting their health in half and switching off their hazards.
-  Perkeo: Short in stature but never short on Passives, this fool has a practically unlimited pool to power him up.  
4 New Weapons & Evolutions
-  Infernolatro: Summon a burning score counter to burn your foolish foes to ashes.  Each enemy you roast fans the flames, expanding the inferno until the whole table's alight.
-  Gros Michel: Release a barrage of banana projectiles, each with a 1-in-6 chance to explode for deliciously critical damage.
-  Fibonacci Spritz: Fires mathematically beautiful spiralling streams of fizzy destruction that can trigger your other weapons.
-  Celestial Booster: Planets and a rocket orbit you constantly, dealing extra damage based on your current riches.
The Ante Chamber
Enter the Ante Chamber, an extradimensional analogue of Balatro's user interface. Battle buffoonish foes on a facsimile of Jimbo's home turf accompanied by a new ally, the  Outer Saboteur, a silly little sidekick that steals your coins to throw them at enemies.
1 New Hypnotically Looping Track
- "Ante Chamber - Balatro Main Theme"
## Trailer
## Trivia
- This is the first DLC wherein the collaborating IP also features Vampire Survivors in some form.
- Before the release of this DLC, three custom face cards featuring three characters from Vampire Survivors were introduced. The face cards depict  Poe Ratcho,  Queen Sigma, and Bisconte Draculó.
- It is the second DLC to be released for free, with the preceding DLC, Emerald Diorama, being the first.
- In the cover art of the DLC, Ben Starr's portrayal of  Jimbo is holding the Vampire joker from Balatro, which references Bisconte Draculó. He is also holding 17 cards from the Red Deck.
- This is the first DLC to add only one new music track, instead of the typical multi-track album. This is in reference to Balatro itself only having one music track which loops.
- The code name for Ante Chamber was "Lemon", from the Italian word "Limonare", which means "To make out".
=====

# Charge
Charge is a mechanic in Vampire Survivors where specific characters have an additional orange meter underneath their health bar which fills up over time. Once the meter is full, weapons specified in the character's description will be forcibly triggered. Some characters require the player to stand still to fill up the Charge meter, while others only build up Charge while moving. Additionally, the amount of time it takes some characters to fill their Charge meter can be reduced by increasing a specified stat.
Charge abilities include:
## Characters
-  Keitha Muort [DLC] fires 7  Flash Arrow projectiles. Charge time affected by  Luck.
-  Jonathan Morris [DLC] triggers all equipped  Coat of Arms weapons. Charge time affected by  Speed.
-  Charlotte Aulin [DLC] triggers all equipped  Belnades' Spellbook weapons. Charge time affected by  Growth.
-  Alucard [DLC] triggers  Soul Steal.
-  Shanoa [DLC] triggers all equipped  Ebony Diabologue weapons.
-  Quincy Morris [DLC] triggers a  Neutron Bomb. Charge time affected by  Might.
-  Carmilla [DLC] triggers  Dominus Hatred,  Dominus Anger, and  Dominus Agony if they are equipped. Charge time affected by  Curse.
-  Diva No. 5 "Battle Tank" [DLC] fires 7  Super Missile projectiles.
-  Diva No. 5 "Shanks" [DLC] triggers the Ballistic Missiles tech.

# Cheat Codes
"Cheats" redirects here. For codes typed into the Secrets menu, see Spells.
Below is a list of Cheat Codes in Vampire Survivors that the player can use for various effects.
Each key must be typed within two seconds. For "pet", it must be typed within half a second. Arrow keys must be used for directional inputs.
Note that all of them require a physical keyboard to be inputted, and are therefore unusable on consoles and mobile versions; Toastie and Smith IV have their own spells to make them obtainable on those platforms.
## List of Cheat Codes
- Unlock Mortaccio and gain 2,800 - In Main Menu, use the Konami Code (up, up, down, down, left, right, left, right, escape, enter)
- Unlock Toastie - After unlocking Exdash, after damaging Stalker, Drowner, and Trickster with Dairy Cart, Rosary, Gorgeous Moon, Infinite Corridor, and Crimson Shroud (Pentagram only in Cappella Magna and they disappear after the Ender fight) and type down and enter when it shows up at the bottom right corner and yells "panini".
- Unlock Smith IV - After unlocking Toastie, type "spam" in the main menu; enters character select menu, type "spam" again; enters stage select menu, type "spam" once more; enter a run, press "humbug", all within 30 seconds.
- Unlock Game Killer (0), Mad Groove (VIII), Out of Bounds (XII), Disco of Gold (XV), and Silent Old Sanctuary (XX) - In Main Menu: "aintnobodygottimeforthat"
- Petting and healing O'Sole - During a run while playing as O'sole: "pet" (works once per run)
## Trivia
- The code to unlock Toastie is a reference to the game Mortal Kombat II, where to fight the hidden character Smoke, players would have to play on a specific stage and wait for Dan Forden to appear and say "Toasty" before pressing Down + Start.
- The code to unlock Smith is a reference to a cheat code in Ultima VI by talking to Iolo, then typing "spam", enter, "spam", enter, "spam", enter, "humbug", and enter.
- The code to unlock the time-gated Arcanas is a reference to a viral YouTube video of Kimberly "Sweet Brown" Wilkins being interviewed after having escaped a fire in an apartment complex.
- The code to pet and heal O'Sole is a reference to petting dogs in video games popularized by the twitter account Can You Pet The Dog? noting which video game allows the player to interact with a dog.
- The account has also noted the code to heal O'Sole for this game.

# Coffin
Sprite of a coffin
Coffins are objects found in certain stages that can be opened to unlock new characters for purchase. Opening any previously-opened coffin will unlock  Random.
## Locations
| Stage | Character | Stage | Character | Stage | Character
| Mad Forest | Pugnala Provola | Inlaid Library | Giovanna Grana | Dairy Plant | Poppea Pecorina
| Gallo Tower | Concetta Caciotta | Cappella Magna | Zi'Assunta Belpaese | Carlo Cart | Gyoruntin
| Astral Stair | Rose De Infernas | Mt. Moonspell | Miang Moonspell | Lake Foscari | Eleanor Uziron
| Polus Replica | Crewmate Dino | Neo Galuga | Bill Rizer | Hectic Highway | Brad Fang
| Ode to Castlevania | Leon BelmontJulius BelmontVlad Tepes DraculaReinhardt Schneider | Emerald Diorama | Tsunanori MidoSiugnas | Ante Chamber | Jimbo
## Opening Coffins
In the Milky Way Map, unopened coffins are marked with a question mark (?) symbol. The arrow pointing at the coffin will use the icon in the achievements.
Upon arriving at the coffin, an expanding and contracting ring of enemies appears around it. Once all of these enemies are defeated, the player can open the coffin by moving over it. A character will emerge and proclaim, "Thank you [player character], but the vampire is in another coffin!"
The rescued character joins the survivors and can be purchased for a minimum of 1,000 (the price scales with the number of coffin characters that have already been purchased).
## Tips
- An effective way to deal with a horde of enemies surrounding a coffin during early game is using the  Pentagram, as it may clear most or all of the enemies surrounding the coffin (depending on which ones are shown on screen at the time the Pentagram is activated).
- Since an upgraded weapon is immediately triggered after exiting the leveling up screen, the Pentagram can be upgraded (if available from the level up options), so that it can be used to defeat the horde more easily.
- The  Clock Lancet can be useful to temporarily freeze some enemies that surround a Coffin.
## Traits
All base game coffin characters share the following traits:
- They are unlocked by interacting with a coffin in a specific stage.
- They say "Thank you (character name), but the vampire is in another coffin." when the player finds them.
- They have alliterative names, except for Zi'Assunta Belpaese.
- They have a kind of cheese as their last name.
- They are female.
- They have an uncapped passive ability bonus.
- They start with +20% Move Speed.
- Each of them has a corresponding background music that plays during a run, regardless of the stage.
- The name of the theme is related to their weapon, except for Concetta and Zi'Assunta.
- Both Concetta and Zi'Assunta also start with +10% Curse, a trait not shared with the other coffin characters.
- They all gain a key component of Omni each level, besides the DLC characters.
- Zi'Assunta just gains Omni each level.
## Audio
When the player opens a coffin, the Character Unlock jingle plays. Then the Character Reveal jingle is played as the found character is revealed.
## Trivia
-  Avatar Infernas is also a character found in a coffin, but is a secret character and the method to unlock him and his traits greatly differ from the other coffin characters.
- Although  Rose De Infernas and  Gyoruntin are secret characters, they still resemble the standard coffin characters in terms of their unlocking method, only with an added pre-requisite that must be fulfilled in order for their coffins to appear.
- The first coffin character introduced in the game was  Poppea Pecorina, who was added in version 0.3.0.
- The line "Thank you (character name), but the vampire is in another coffin." is a reference to Toad's infamous line in the original Super Mario Bros. ("Thank You Mario, But Our Princess is in Another Castle").

# Critical Hit
[A whip is used on a bat enemy on the Mad Forest stage, one of the enemies hit receives 7 damage in a damage text that appears white, the other receives 43 damage in a damage text that appears red. The one that appears red is a Critical Hit.]A critical hit with Whip while using the Slash (XVI) arcana. The crit appears red compared to the normal hit.
Critical Hit (sometimes referred to as "crit") is a special attack type in Vampire Survivors. It allows a weapon to roll a Critical Hit Chance on hit and if succeeded apply a Critical Hit Damage Multiplier to the damage that would be dealt. Not all weapons are capable of Critical Hits. 
Critical Hit Chance is affected by  Luck.
Formula for Luck's effect on Critical Hit Chance: chanceWithLuck = positiveEffectChance ⋅ totalLuck
(Critical Hit Chance shares the same percentage with other chance based effects a.k.a positiveEffectChance (there are a few exceptions))
(totalLuck = luck percentage + 100, so 30% luck = 130 totalLuck, and 130 totalLuck ⋅ 10% critical chance = 13% critical chance) 
## Effects
Different weapons have different Critical Hit Chances and Critical Hit Multipliers.
 Slash (XVI) doubles Critical Hit Multipliers and provides Critical Hit Chance to select weapons. 
 Claimh Solais [DLC] passively increases Critical Hit Damage by up to 100% depending on the amount of enemies on screen. This effect is applied to all equipped weapons that are capable of Critical Hits.
The following list includes weapons that are able to Critical Hit:

# CriticalHP
CriticalHP is a mechanic in Vampire Survivors. When a characters falls below 20% of their  Max Health, they will trigger all CriticalHP skills. CriticalHP skills will not be able to trigger this way again until the player has fully healed.
Acquiring or leveling up the  Velvet Dodecahedron [DLC] will forcibly trigger all CriticalHP skills.
## Characters
-  Bat Robbert: Heals 30% of his Max Health and gains +32  Max Health
-  Santa Ladonna: Activation of a  Rosary
-  She-Moon Eeta: Activation of a  Orologion
-  Para Kooleo: Activation of a  Vacuum
-  Malice Bloodmoon [DLC]: Activation of her Blood Hex
-  Ashtart [DLC]: +1  Revival
-  Congregation [DLC]: Revival-based activations of their main weapon.
-  In' Kujata [DLC]: +50  Armor for 10 seconds.
-  Simondo Belmont [DLC]: Summons extra  Rapid Fires [DLC],  Grenades [DLC], and  Barriers [DLC]
-  Alucard [DLC]: Activates his hidden  Sword Brothers [DLC]
-  Eric Lecarde [DLC]: Activates  Burning Alcarde [DLC]
-  Shaft [DLC]: Activates  Shaft Orb [DLC]
- Activation of Showstopper for most characters from Emerald Diorama
## Arcana
-  Crystal Cries (XII): Activates an  Orologion
-  Heir of Fate (XIX): Activates an  Nduja Fritta Tanto
## Survarots
-  Arca Ladonna: Triggers an  Nduja Fritta Tanto
-  Poe Ratcho: Gains 40  Max Health and recovers 30% Health
- Skill: Triggers an  Nduja Fritta Tanto
- Skill: +50  Armor for 10 seconds
- Skill: Gains 40  Max Health and recovers 30% Health

# Directer's Cut
Directer's Cut is the name used for the development version of Vampire Survivors. It has been shown in one official teaser video and one unofficial video from the 2023 Co-op London Party.
## Videos
=====
# Emerald Diorama
This article is about the DLC. For other uses, see Emerald Diorama (disambiguation).
| Emerald Diorama
| 
| Expansion
| Release date | 10 April 2025 (Update)
| Platform(s) | Steam, Xbox, PlayStation, Nintendo Switch, Epic Game Store, iOS, Android
| Retail price | Free
| Theme | SaGa: Emerald Beyond
| {"image":"Emerald Diorama.png","name":"Emerald Diorama","theme":"SaGa: Emerald Beyond","release":"10 April 2025","price":"Free","update":"Vampire Survivors: Emerald Diorama is free and out now","platform":"Steam, Xbox, PlayStation, Nintendo Switch, Epic Game Store, iOS, Android"}
In a universe where a single level up choice can change everything, the SaGa: Emerald Beyond characters stand shoulder to shoulder with the Vampire Survivors, united to face their next adventure together. Will the Vampire Survivors be graced by the presence of a vampire? We doubt it, but never say never.
Official description
Emerald Diorama is the sixth expansion for Vampire Survivors, released on 10 April 2025. It is the first free expansion to the game. It is themed around the role-playing video game SaGa: Emerald Beyond.
## Features
12+ New Characters
An eclectic cast of bizarre SaGa protagonists join the Survivors, including:
-  Tsunanori Mido - A fate-forged master of Kugutsu puppets.
-  Ameya Aisling - An undercover witch searching for her lost magic (and cats)
-  Bonnie Blair &  Formina Franklyn - A duo of daring detectives who fight side-by-side with fists and firearms.
-  Diva No. 5 - A songstress mech forsaken for singing a forbidden melody, travelling between different worlds (and robotic bodies) in search of her heart.
- And Many More! - including a teacher of heroes, an Emperor unthawed, and a Dismal King with some bloodthirsty tendencies...
16+ New Weapons & the Glimmer mechanic
40 new ways to attack, including base weapons, evolutions, and the sensational Glimmer mechanic from the SaGa series. Whenever you attack with these weapons, there's a chance to spontaneously learn a special technique through the Glimmer system. Swords, magics, punches, and missiles: all the variety of SaGa gets represented!
-  Fleuret - stab enough encroaching enemies and you'll Glimmer the Crystalline Carve, letting you freeze foes whilst cutting them down.
-  Pressure Point - unleash a barrage of martial arts blows at close range to Glimmer the far-reaching fire jab Shenlong Firefist.
-  Hecaton Machine Gun - shoot a blistering salvo of interestingly inaccurate bullets to Glimmer the Bombarding Fire technique, blasting baddies with a massive upheaval of the earth.
-  Hyperion Bazooka - master these high-power missiles that explode as they penetrate through hordes of foes to Glimmer the Cosmic Rave to massively multiply your firepower.
Character parties
No JRPG DLC is complete without a squad of odd allies to pal around with. Level up Tsunanori Mido to summon up to 4 Kugutsu puppets, fighting independently with their own weapons in a defensive cross formation. Pick either Bonnie or Formina to play as both intrepid investigators, combining punches and pistols for a powerful partnership. And in the darkness of Yomi, rumours swirl of a Dismal King who can enthrall defeated foes to make his own army of allies…
Showstopper
Survivors never give up! When a SaGa character reaches critical HP they fight back from the brink with a Showstopper, a brief burst of bonus Might, decreased Cooldown, and a little extra Luck to help them survive whatever precarious situation you’ve put them in with a tempest of powered-up projectiles.   
A New World-Weaving Stage
Follow the Emerald Wave through a diorama of interwoven worlds, the perfect setting to walk sideways while mowing down masses of unusual monsters and bonkers bosses. 
Remixes of Stupendous SaGa Emerald Beyond Tracks 
🎵"A Fierce Engagement - VS version"
🎵"A Quick Warm-Up - VS version"
🎵"Against All Odds - VS version"
🎵"CCPD, Moving Out! - VS version"
🎵"Darkness Incarned - VS version"
🎵"Emerald Destinies - VS version"
🎵"Feel the Beat - VS version"
🎵"The World's Heartbeat - VS version"
🎵"Yumeha, Chipper as Ever! - VS version"

## Trivia
- The reason why this DLC was made is because Luca Galante, the creator of Vampire Survivors and the head of poncle, is a long time fan of the SaGa games. This prompted poncle to reach out to Square Enix, the owners of the SaGa IP, to include content from SaGa: Emerald Beyond onto Vampire Survivors, which Square Enix allowed poncle to do so.

# Emergency Meeting
This article is about the DLC. For other uses, see Emergency Meeting (disambiguation).
| Emergency Meeting
| 
| Expansion
| Release date | 18 December 2023 (Update)
| Platform(s) | PC, Xbox, PlayStation, Nintendo Switch, iOS, Android

| Theme | Among Us
| {"image":"Emergency Meeting.png","name":"Emergency Meeting","theme":"Among Us","release":"18 December 2023","price":"$2.49","update":"DLC & Patch 1.8.206","platform":"PC, Xbox, PlayStation, Nintendo Switch, iOS, Android"}
Gather your most trusted Crewmates, prepare to eject the Impostors, and try to survive while mowing down thousands of extra-terrestrial foes in this expansion for Vampire Survivors. Turn classic Among Us tasks into outrageous weapons, upgrading your abilities after every roguelite run to overcome impossible odds with ease. Be alert to subterfuge as you explore the vast weirdness of the Polus Replica and keep your wits about you, as suspicious eyes are always watching 👀.
Official description
Emergency Meeting is the third expansion for Vampire Survivors, released on December 18, 2023. It is themed around Among Us by Innersloth, a co-op social deduction game where players are tasked with finding and ejecting the imposters.
This makes it the first DLC that is revolved around a video game IP outside of Vampire Survivors.
## Features
- 9 totally trustworthy new characters:
-  Crewmate Dino- Starry-eyed, optimistic, and unfortunately extremely tasty. Dino is a jack-of-all-trades who boosts stats whenever a task is completed (i.e. when you level up a weapon).
-  Engineer Gino- Few Crewmates come as close to truly competent task-work as Gino, who offers a selection of passive weapons from their toolbox every few levels.
-  Ghost Lino- Good Crewmates don't let a silly thing like death get in the way of their tasks. Lino can float through walls at will and can't be hurt, but can't deal damage either... what could be the goal of such suspicious game design?!
-  Shapeshifter Nino- Look at this bean! They can do all kinds of... wait, where did they go? And where did this snowman come from? Oh well, I'm sure you'll figure it out.
-  Guardian Pina- Everyone needs an angel on their shoulder. Pina isn't just there for moral support, though - they boost the defences of all nearby allies, and they love taking out the garbage.
-  Impostor Rina- A fellow Crewmate that is in no way some kind of duplicitous, well-disguised killer. One hundred percent all-natural-bean, right here. Nothing to see or think about.
-  Scientist Mina- Mina's scientific skill is matched only by their intense clumsiness. Thankfully, dropping potentially hazardous chemicals has a major upside when you're besieged by extra-terrestrial monsters.
-  Horse- It's a horse. Neigh, etc.
-  Megalo Impostor Rina
- 15 new weapons:
-  Report!
-  Emergency Meeting
-  Lucky Swipe
-  Crossed Wires
-  Science Rocks
-  Rocket Science
-  Lifesign Scan
-  Paranormal Scan
-  Clear Debris
-  Clear Asteroids
-  Sharp Tongue( Silver Tongue)
-  Impostongue
-  Just Vent
-  Unjust Ejection
-  Hats
- 8 new passive items:
-  Mini Crewmate
-  Mini Engineer
-  Mini Ghost
-  Mini Shapeshifter
-  Mini Guardian
-  Mini Impostor
-  Mini Scientist
-  Mini Horse
- 6 new music tracks:
- "Emergency Meeting"
- "Hide and Survive"
- "No Ducks Among Us"
- "No Pirates Among Us"
- "No Ghosts Among Us"
- "No Vampires Among Us"

- Polus Replica
- 1 new adventure

# Expansions
After the release of the 1.0 version of Vampire Survivors, the developers have worked on including new expansions/DLCs for the game. Each of those expansions introduce new and exclusive characters, weapons, stages, and more.
To date, there are 8 expansions that have been introduced to the game.
## Expansions list
Note: The DLCs are listed by release date and not by in-game order.
## Legacy of the Moonspell
Main article: Legacy of the Moonspell
Legacy of the Moonspell is the first DLC introduced to the game. It was released on 15 December 2022. It introduces 8 characters, 13 weapons, a new passive item, a new stage, a new relic, an adventure, and 6 music tracks.
## Tides of the Foscari
Main article: Tides of the Foscari
Tides of the Foscari is the second DLC introduced to the game. It was released on 13 April 2023. It introduces 8 characters, 13 weapons, a new passive item, two stages (Lake Foscari and Abyss Foscari), a new relic, an adventure, and 7 music tracks.
## Emergency Meeting
Main article: Emergency Meeting
Emergency Meeting is the third DLC introduced to the game. It was released on 18 December 2023 and is themed around Among Us, making it the first DLC to be themed on a video game IP outside of Vampire Survivors. It introduces 9 characters, 15 weapons, 9 passive items, a new stage, an adventure, and 6 music tracks.
## Operation Guns
Main article: Operation Guns
Operation Guns is the fourth DLC introduced to the game. It was released on 9 May 2024 and is themed around Contra. It introduces 12 characters, 22 weapons, a new passive item, 3 new pickups, two stages (Neo Galuga and Hectic Highway), an adventure, and 19 music tracks.
## Ode to Castlevania
Main article: Ode to Castlevania
Ode to Castlevania is the fifth DLC introduced to the game. It was released on 31 October 2024 and is themed around Castlevania. It is the largest and most expensive DLC to date, having introduced 96 characters (40 standard characters and 56 secret characters), 126 weapons, 6 special weapon selectors, 5 new pickups, a massive stage of the same name, an adventure, 11 new relics, and 103 music tracks.
## Emerald Diorama
Main article: Emerald Diorama
Emerald Diorama is the sixth DLC introduced to the game. It was released on 10 April 2025 and is themed around SaGa. It is the first free expansion introduced to the game. It introduces 12 new characters, 16 new weapons, character parties, the Glimmer and Showstopper mechanics, a new stage, a new relic, and 9 music tracks.
## Ante Chamber
Main article: Ante Chamber
Ante Chamber is the seventh DLC introduced to the game. It was released on 28 October 2025 and is themed around Balatro. It is the second free expansion introduced to the game. It introduces 4 new characters, 8 new weapons, a new passive item, a new stage, a new relic, and a single music track.
## Legacy of the Bloodmoon
Main article: Legacy of the Bloodmoon
Legacy of the Bloodmoon is the eighth DLC introduced to the game. It was released on August 28 2026. It introduces 12 new characters, 16 new weapons, a new passive item, an adventure, a new stage, and 11 music tracks.
## DLC Store
The in-game DLC store in the mobile version of the game.
In the main menu, if a mobile player wants to purchase a DLC, they will have to tap on the green "DLC STORE" button that is listed just above the blue "START" button to access the DLC store. After that, the player will be able to select a DLC that they want to purchase.

# Flight
Not to be confused with Wall Clipping.
Flight is a mechanic specific to some characters in Vampire Survivors. This is typically noted in the character's description by "ignores walls" or "can fly", but may be omitted in some cases.

# Freeze
Clock Lancet freezing enemies.
Freeze or Time Freeze is an effect that completely stops affected enemies. Frozen enemies turn blue and are unable to move and attack. However, the player can still deal damage to frozen enemies and push them around by walking into them. Some larger enemies and stage bosses are resistant or immune to the freeze effect.
The following items can apply freeze:
- Weapons:
-  Clock Lancet and  Infinite Corridor
-  Celestial Voulge has a 5% chance (modified by Luck) of critically hitting and freezing enemies hit.
-  Mirage Robe [DLC] and  J'Odore [DLC]
-  Prism Lass [DLC],  Prism Damsel [DLC],  Time Warp [DLC]
-  Icebrand [DLC] and  Ninth Circle [DLC]
-  Fleuret [DLC],  Dress Sword [DLC] and  Espada Ropera [DLC]
- The Arcana Jail of Crystal (XIV) grants the following weapons a 25% chance to freeze, modified by Luck, for an amount of time depending on that weapon's Duration:
-  Magic Wand and  Holy Wand
-  Runetracer and  NO FUTURE
-  Eight The Sparrow and  Twice Upon a Time
-  Cygnus
-  Bracelet,  Bi-Bracelet, and  Tri-Bracelet
-  Prismatic Missile [DLC] and  Luminaire [DLC]
-  C-U-Laser [DLC] and  Pronto Beam [DLC]
-  Dragon Water Whip [DLC] and  Hydrostormer Tip [DLC]
-  Ice Fang [DLC] and its counterpart Speculo Ice Fang, as well as  Cocytus [DLC].
-  Hydra Cannon [DLC] and  Hyperion Bazooka [DLC]
-  Spirit Rings [DLC] and  Rings of Calamity [DLC]
- Pickups
-  Orologions freeze all enemies, including any newly spawned ones, for 10 seconds when collected.
-  Out of Bounds (XII) doubles the spawn rate of Orologions by doubling its spawn weight. Upon freezing an enemy, it also create an explosion dealing damage to it and nearby enemies.
-  Crystal Cries (XII) gives the player an Orologion that activates once their Health is depleted to 20%, and then re-arms once the player regains 100% of their Max Health. Defeating frozen enemies also drops  Crystallized Souls that grant Growth, Recovery, and Max Health when collected.
-  Hail from the Future (X) can generate an Orologion once obtained and when the player levels up.
-  Mad Groove (VIII) gathers every pickup, including Orologions in a circle around the player once obtained and every 2 minutes afterward.
-  Sorbettos fire a cone of icy wind in front of the character that freezes all enemies hit for 10 seconds.
-  Gemini (I) causes Sorbettos to fire a second cone behind the character.
## Freeze resistance
Freeze resistance can prevent the enemy from being frozen when they are hit by the player's weapons. The global freeze from Orologions are unaffected by freeze resistance.
If the target enemy's freeze resistance is strictly higher than the freeze chance of the weapon (1 for Clock Lancet/Infinite Corridor, 0.25 for Jail of Crystal weapons), it will not be affected by the freeze effect. Luck does not increase freeze chance for this comparison.
 Garlic and  Soul Eater can lower an enemy's resistance to freeze. If the enemy is not resistant to debuffs, their freeze resistance is reduced by 0.1 every time they are hit by these weapons until their freeze resistance reaches zero.

# Gate Key
DLC Content (Ode to Castlevania)
This article primarily features content only available in DLC, which may not be available in all copies of the game.
Gate Keys are Relics obtained in the Ode to Castlevania DLC. There are six different Gate Keys, and they are all obtained from defeating a Boss in the stage. Each key grants deeper access into Dracula's Castle, as well as unlocking a teleporter in the area. Along with more areas to explore, obtaining a key unlocks Pickups, Weapons, or new bosses.
| List of Gate Keys
| Icon | Name | Description | Boss | Unlocks
| | Stallion Gate | Allows Heart Refreshes to materalise and grants access to a new part of Dracula's Castle. | Giant Bat | Heart Refresh
| | Scorpion Gate | Allows Karma Coin to materialise and grants access to a new part of Dracula's Castle. | Puppet Master | Karma Coin
| | Capra Gate | Allows Mirrors of Truth to materialise and grants access to a new part of Dracula's Castle. | Paranoia | Mirror of Truth
| | King's Gate | Allows Neutron Bombs to materialise and grants access to a new part of Dracula's Castle. | Abbadon | Neutron Bomb
| | Serpent Gate | Allows clockwork weapons to materialise and grants access to a new part of Dracula's Castle. | Menace | Endo Gears Peri Pendulum Myo Lift Epi Head
| | Beast Gate | Grants access to a new part of Dracula's Castle. | Eligor | N/A
## Trivia
-  Megalo Death's dying animation cycles through the images of the Gates.

# Glimmers
DLC Content (Emerald Diorama)
This article primarily features content only available in DLC, which may not be available in all copies of the game.
Glimmers are a mechanic added to Vampire Survivors in the Emerald Diorama DLC, which is borrowed from SaGa.
When using weapons from the Emerald Diorama DLC, there is a chance based on the character's  Luck stat that a lightbulb will appear above their head before an attack, signaling that a Glimmer has been triggered. The weapon will then fire a flashy new technique, as well as its standard attack.
By default, an Emerald Diorama weapon does not fire its Glimmer Attacks and must first "Glimmer" to unlock the Glimmer Attack associated with its evolution. Evolved weapons also have access to the Glimmer Attacks of their respective base weapon, and 3rd evolutions have access to both the evolved and base weapon's Glimmer Attacks.
All Glimmer Attacks will trigger from every weapon activation during Showstopper.
The list of glimmers that are recently triggered are swiftly shown in a tech name carousel, which can be found around the right side of the HUD. In the Options menu and the Pause menu, the player has the option disable and/or (re-)enable for the tech name carousel to be shown in the screen.
Unlike traditional weapons, most glimmer techs do not have an independent weapon ID and are instead properties of the weapon they're attached to. However, this is not the case for all glimmers. Those which do have their own independent ID are listed below.

# Gold Coin (currency)
Coin
This article is about the currency. For the pickup, see Gold Coin (pickup).
Gold Coins are the basic currency in the game and are used for purchasing PowerUps, new characters, and purchasing items and services from vendors such as the  Merchant and  Master Librarian [DLC].
The collected amount of gold coins is depicted on the top center area of the game's interface, particularly when the player is currently not playing a round.
## Obtaining
Gold Coins can be obtained as pickups in the form of the  Gold Coin,  Coin Bag, or  Rich Coin Bag from destroyed light sources. Opening Treasure Chests will always give a random amount of Gold Coins between 60 and 500. If none of the items in the player's inventory can be upgraded, new Chests will award extra coins instead, and once the inventory is full and maxed out, level ups will give the player an option for extra Coins. Gold Coins are also awarded when the player finishes the run just after being killed by  The Reaper.
The amount of Gold Coins earned can be increased with  Greed stat, which can be gained from the PowerUp of the same name (up to +50%) and the  Stone Mask (up to +50%) passive item. Additionally, the Hyper mode's modifiers in any stage further increase the amount of gold gained (x1.5 - x2 multiplier). With aforementioned upgrades, the player can gain up to 4x the original amount of coins.
Gold Coins have a visual cap of 9,999,999. The true amount of gold obtained is only visible in game and in the results screen. The internal amount of Gold Coins is hard capped at 3.402823 E+38, which is the 32-bit floating point maximum.
## Adventures
In Adventures mode, the amount of gold coins collected in each adventure is independent from that of the main game and other adventures.
A key feature in Adventures is Planar Bazaar, a place where players can spend coins to unlock the availability of particular weapons and passives in their current adventure. These weapons and passives are offered by various characters who serve as merchants in the adventure.
If a player spends their coins on unlocking or buying a weapon or passive item in a different stage, they will have that item equipped for that stage.

# Health Drain
Health Drain is a special weapon effect in Vampire Survivors. Weapons with Health Drain effect will slightly damage the character that holds it every second. Certain characters, as well as Invulnerable characters, are immune to Health Drain.
## Effect
Health Drain can trigger all upon-damaged effect, like Retaliate.
The following weapons have Health Drain effect:
-  Muramasa [DLC]
-  Dominus Anger [DLC],  Dominus Hatred [DLC],  Dominus Agony [DLC] and  Power of Sire [DLC]
The following Characters are immune to Health Drain:
-  Vlad Tepes Dracula [DLC] and  Megalo Dracula [DLC]
-  Alucard [DLC]
-  Soma Cruz [DLC]
-  Stella Lecarde [DLC] and  Loretta Lecarde [DLC]
-  Graham Jones [DLC]
-  Joachim Armster [DLC]
-  Walter Bernhard [DLC]
-  Stone Skull [DLC]
-  Carmilla [DLC]
-  Jiangshi [DLC]
-  Death (character) [DLC] and  Megalo Death [DLC]
-  Siugnas [DLC]

# Hidden weapons
Hidden weapons are weapons characters use innately without being displayed or taking up a weapon slot. Some of these hidden weapons may have reskinned visual effects, such as the  Thousand Edge that  Malphas uses, which is displayed as black feathers rather than knives.

# Invulnerable
Invulnerable is a stat in Vampire Survivors. When Invulnerable is active, the character cannot take damage until Invulnerable ends.
Invulnerable's associated passive item is  Parm Aegis, which provides extended invulnerability time upon taking damage.
The following weapons can provide the Invulnerable stat:
-  Laurel and  Crimson Shroud upon taking damage
-  Sword Brothers [DLC] and  Vjaya Sisters [DLC]
-  Sacred Beasts Tower Shield [DLC] upon overhealing
-  Jetstream [DLC] potentially upon dealing critical damage
-  Road to Heaven [DLC] after the 108th activation
Upon being Invulnerable, the following effects will be triggered:
-  Embrace of Gaea gains increased damage
-  Stellar Blade [DLC] deals critical damage
-  Vincent Dorin [DLC] gains double Greed
-  Hammer [DLC] gains double Amount
The following characters can become Invulnerable temporarily:
-  Menya Moonspell [DLC]
-  Impostor Rina [DLC]
-  Eric Lecarde [DLC]
-  Shaft [DLC]
The following characters are permanently Invulnerable, but will be killed upon reaching the stage time limit:
-  Megalo Menya Moonspell [DLC]
-  Ghost Lino [DLC]
-  Young Maria Renard [DLC]
-  Megalo Death [DLC]
-  Megalo Sargon [DLC]
-  Baal'Thamut [DLC]
- Beside being Invulnerable,  Gekkojin [DLC] will heal by the amount of damage taken, which are counted as Overhealing.
=====
# Knockback
Knockback is an effect applied to enemies that briefly pushes them backwards whenever they are damaged by the player.
When an enemy is receiving knockback, its overall movement speed is reversed and multiplied by a knockback multiplier for the duration of the effect. The knockback multiplier is the product of the damaging weapon's knockback dealt multiplier and knockback taken multiplier of the target enemy. The knockback effect lasts 120 milliseconds.[Verify]
Garlic and its evolution Soul Eater have an additional effect that increases the knockback taken by enemies hit by it. If the enemy is not immune to debuffs, every time it is hit by either of these weapons, its knockback taken multiplier is increased by an additional 0.3 until its maximum knockback value is reached.
=====
# Last Breath
Last Breath is a mechanic in Vampire Survivors. When a character reaches 1 health, they will trigger their "Last Breath" skills. Most Last Breath skills also include a period of invulnerability or healing. Last Breath skills can normally only be triggered once per life, but can be triggered multiple times in the same life by  Velvet Dodecahedron [DLC].
Last Breath skills include:
## Characters
-  Genevieve Gruyère [DLC] and  Je-Ne-Viv [DLC]'s World Eater.
-  Baal'Thasar [DLC]'s blood gem-based  Might and  Cooldown bonuses.
-  Calogero Bloodmoon [DLC]'s +50%  Max Health boost.
-  Congregation [DLC]'s  Revival-based weapon activations.
-  Malice Bloodmoon [DLC]'s activation of her Blood Hex.
-  Nameless Fool [DLC]'s acquisition of a Darkana.
-  Sara Trantoul [DLC]'s activation of a  Nduja Fritta Tanto.
-  Soma Cruz [DLC]'s activation of  Soul Steal [DLC].

In ancient times, Saint Baal'Thasar had a vision of paradise, lit by a bright, red moon. And so he founded the Order of the Red Moon, to lead people to a land of eternal life. The myth says that Baal'Thasar found his paradise during a pilgrimage, and so never returned.
Having lost its leader, the Order shattered and many fringe sects were born from its remnants. Today, members of one of the most unscrupulous cults, Clan Bloodmoon, embarks on a journey to follow in Baal'Thasar's footsteps...
Official description
Legacy of the Bloodmoon is the eighth expansion for Vampire Survivors, and was released on 28 August 2026. It is themed as the evil twin of the Legacy of the Moonspell expansion, and was originally planned as an update to the Legacy of Moonspell DLC but later became a larger project expanding to become its own DLC. It is the third expansion that is not based on an external game or topic.
This expansion pack follows Clan Bloodmoon as they retrace the pilgrimage of Saint Baal'Thasar, in search of a paradise lit by a bright red moon. There is also an ancient manor that may or may not hide the secret of immortality.

In eastern lands, a clan has fallen. The Moonspell, once vigilant guardians of a sorcerous valley nestled in the mountains, have been overrun by hordes of yokai and oni. Though treacherous, this hive of spectral activity may provide some clue as to the location of a vampire. If not, at least it’ll be entertaining to defeat thousands of wayward spirits in the process.
Official description
Legacy of the Moonspell is the first expansion DLC for Vampire Survivors, released on December 15, 2022. It adds a new stage, Mt.Moonspell, new characters, and new weapons. It is themed around the Moonspell clan and the mysterious powers that they hold. With version 1.16 Legacy of the Moonspell was updated with new characters, weapons, music and buffs to existing characters in the expansion.
## Features
- 8 new characters:
-  Miang Moonspell - The last disciple of the Moonspell Clan before its downfall, and possibly its finest. Self-consciously a shounen protagonist.
-  Menya Moonspell - One of the few surviving Moonspell elders, Menya’s mystical powers are near god-like.
-  Syuuto Moonspell - Banished practitioner of the new moon dark magic, Syuuto is nonetheless an enemy of evil. Poor personal hygiene may contribute to his continued exile.
-  Babi-Onna - Returned from the dead to seek vengeance on demons and mortals alike, she nonetheless retains her impeccable ability to charm, amuse, and dazzle.
-  McCoy-Oni
-  Gav'Et-Oni ★
-  Megalo Menya Moonspell
-  Megalo Syuuto Moonspell
- 13 new weapons:
-  Silver Wind - An ancestral force unleashed by the staff of the Moonspell Clan, only those born under the moon can fully wield its power (or whoever finds it in a chest, we’re not sure).
-  Festive Winds
-  Four Seasons - A set of orbs which unleash the power of the changing seasons, of death and rebirth.
-  Godai Shuffle
-  Summon Night - A weapon which drips with the darkness of the new moon. Despite the clan’s superstitions, even the shadows can oppose evil.
-  Echo Night
-  Mirage Robe - An enchanted kimono, weaved on a loom from the silk of the earth spider, and imbued with a fragment of Babi-Onna’s vengeful spirit. Besides, it looks fabulous darling.
-  J'Odore
-  Night Sword
-  Muramasa
-  Mille Bolle Blu
-  Boo Roo Boolle
-  108 Bocce
- 6 new music tracks:
- "Legacy of the Moonspell"
- "Festive Breeze"
- "Rumble Kachara"
- "Haze of Night"
- "Hell Night"
- "Crystal Allure"
- 1 new stage:
- Mt.Moonspell - Our biggest stage yet, a sprawling map featuring several different environments, all with their own challenges and resident monsters! This one stage includes an abandoned castle, snow covered mountain, and a yokai infested village, and more besides.
- 1 new adventure

# Level up
An example of a level up bar, as shown on the top of the game HUD.
An example of a level up screen. Here, the player has leveled up and is given the choice between three items: Eight The Sparrow, the Crown, and Flames of Misspell.
Note:  Rerolls,  Skips, and/or  Banishes become available in this screen if the player invests in these PowerUps and/or uses a character that may start with these characteristics.
When the player collects enough experience, they will gain a level up. Each successive level up requires more experience than the previous. Upon leveling up, the game is paused and the player is given 3 or 4 unique options consisting of weapons and passive items to choose from. If the player chooses an item they do not have, it will be added to their inventory. If they already have the chosen item, the item will be upgraded to its next level. After selecting an item, the game resumes and the character gains a brief moment of invulnerability.
The player cannot be offered new items if the already have 6 different passive items or weapons. Similarly, if an item is at its maximum level, it also cannot be offered to the player. When both of these conditions are met and player has nothing left to upgrade, level ups will instead grant the options to gain extra Gold or restore health with Floor Chicken, with the option to always receive either Floor Chickens or Gold once the player has received 100  Big Coin Bags from any source.
- The "Always Floor Chicken" option will only give Floor Chickens if the player has missing health. If the player is fully healed, they will receive Gold instead.
Alternatively, if a player has nothing left to upgrade, but Limit Breaking is active, level ups will then offer extra levels to already maxed weapons.
If the player has invested in the Skip or Reroll PowerUps, they will also have the option to skip the item selection and retain some experience or shuffle the current items for a new set. Skips and Rerolls are unavailable when the player has maxed out their inventory.
Upon obtaining the  Brave Story, Random LevelUp is added as an option in the Stage Selection menu. When it is toggled on, each level up randomly chooses an option for the player.
## Mechanics
## Experience requirement
The player starts at level 1 and has to collect 5 XP to level up to level 2. Thereafter, the requirement increases by 10 XP each level until level 20 (i.e. 15 XP is required to go from level 2 to 3, 25 XP from 3 to 4 and so on). From level 21 to 40 the requirement increases by 13 XP each level, and from level 41 onwards the requirement increases by 16 XP each level.
Additionally, at levels 20 and 40 an additional amount of XP – 600 and 2400 respectively – is required to level up to the next level. However, at these levels the player also gains +100% Growth, increasing their experience gain, until they reach the next level.

## Random selection of items
Each item in the item pool has weight that is specified by their rarity value. The weight of the whole item pool is the sum of the rarity values of eligible items. Three (or four) items are then selected from the pool at random without repetitions (i.e. an item cannot be offered more than once per level up). The rough probability of an option to be a certain item is:
P(item)=itemRarityitemPoolWeight
Currently, the total weight of the weapon pool with all DLCs is 8,130 and the passive item pool with all DLCs is 1,370 (total 9,500). Rough probabilities of receiving a certain item of a rarity value can be found in the table below.
| Rarity | ~P(item)
| 1 | 0.011 %
| 40 | 0.421 %
| 50 | 0.526 %
| 60 | 0.632 %
| 70 | 0.737 %
| 80 | 0.842 %
| 90 | 0.947 %
| 100 | 1.053 %
## Receiving owned items
If the player's inventory is not full, there is first an attempt to specifically offer items already owned by the player. The chance to be offered items already in their inventory is affected by Luck and can be calculated as:
ownedChance=1+0.3x−1totalLuck
where x=2 if the player's level is even and x=1 if the level is odd.
The check is performed twice and, if successful, an item already owned by the player will be randomly selected to appear as an option for each successful check. However, if the second roll selects the same item as the first it is discarded and a new item is pulled from the pool of all items, as the same item is not be offered more than once in the same level up.
## Fourth option
It is possible to sometimes gain four item choices upon leveling up. This depends on the character's Luck. The chance to receive a fourth option is:
chanceFourth=1−(1totalLuck)
## Trivia
- When the player levels up, there is an animation showcasing experience gems falling from the top side of the screen.
- If the level up options include  Gatti Amari, cats will replace the experience gems falling from the top of the screen. This is likely a reference to the phrase "raining cats and dogs"
=====
# Light source
Light source
Light sources are destructibles that randomly appear within stages and drop pickups when destroyed. Light sources have 10 health.  Luck increases the spawn rate of light sources and the chance to receive rare pickups.
Destroying 20 light sources unlocks the  Fire Wand.
## Types
Light sources have a unique look depending on the stage.

## Spawn mechanics
Aside for using Mad Groove (VIII) Arcana and Greatest Jubilee weapon, Light sources spawn alongside other destructible, such as Dairy Cart and Stained Glass.
All destructibles spawn barely off-screen.
In some stages, such as Inlaid Library, Dairy Plant and Gallo Tower, the light sources have predetermined possible spawn locations, whereas in other maps they spawn in an oval shape around the character. 
The game attempts to spawn destructibles every second in all stages, except The Bone Zone (every 0.5 seconds) and Holy Forbidden (100 seconds). 
Worth noting: Light sources can spawn on top of each other.
The chance to spawn a destructible is affected by  Luck and can be calculated as follows:
destructibleSpawnChance=mapDestructibleChance⋅totalLuck
The chance (with Luck) cannot exceed the stage's maximum chance. 
Light source spawn attempts won't cease once the maximum existing Light Source capacity has been reached: If a light source is spawned at the limit, a new one will spawn in a location closest to the player, while the oldest existing light source will be despawned. As long as the maximum count remains capped, the spawn chance is not influenced by Luck, consequently severely reducing chances for a new Light Source in this scenario. 
The chances for each stage are listed in the following table.

# Limit Break
An example of a level up screen where the Limit Break mechanic is applied. Here, the  Death Spiral and  La Borra are both evolved weapons that normally do not have more than just a single level. However, with Limit Break, these weapons can now be indefinitely leveled up with more stats, such as additional  Area and  Might.
Limit Break is a game mechanic toggle in Vampire Survivors that allows for upgrading weapons and adding to their stats beyond their maximum level. It is unlocked by obtaining the  Great Gospel.
## Mechanics
When Limit Break is enabled, a fully upgraded build will not be offered  Big Coin Bags or  Floor Chicken upon level up, and instead will present options to level their weapons beyond their usual limits, i.e. their original maximum level or cap imposed from  Banishes after it was obtained. Each option will offer to upgrade a specific stat of one of the player's weapons, and most stats can stack indefinitely.
When a specific bonus stat has a cap, the cap is for the base value of the weapon, not the bonus. For example, maxed  Knife or  Thousand Edge has a pierce of 3. With a cap of 10, they can only take 7 pierce upgrades before it will not be available anymore.
After reaching Limit Break levels, weapons can still be evolved. However, all the extra stats will be reset and the evolved weapon starts from level 1.
There are two options to pick level ups randomly, one is "RANDOM once" and the other is "RANDOM always." The "RANDOM always" option acts similarly to collecting Big Coin Bags or Floor Chicken, where it is automatically collected and the text "Level Up!" appears above the character. The stat appears with a similar effect as collecting a  Golden Egg.
If the player collects stage items when they have already started Limit Breaking weapons will temporarily halt the leveling of Limit Break weapons. The leveling of Limit Break weapons resumes if the stage items' levels have been maxed out or disabled by using Banishes.
Limit Break does not affect  Treasure Chests in any way, as they will continue to give  Big Coin Bags as normal.

## Trivia
- The name is a reference to the recurring Limit Break abilities, which allow the characters to use a more powerful attack or utility, or the ability to exceed damage and stat caps in Final Fantasy series.
- Most prominently, it is a reference to Final Fantasy VII and one of its playable characters, Aerith Gainsborough, whose fourth Limit Break, "Great Gospel", has the same name as the relic that unlocks this mechanic in Vampire Survivors.
- In patch 0.9.0, Projectile Speed cap is reduced from 500% to 300% and various Duration cap is added for each weapon affected by it.
- In patch 1.0, the bonus for Base Damage and Area is halved, Area and Amount is capped at 1000% and 20 respectively, and rarity of Chance increased from 1 to 10.
=====
# Main menu
The main menu encountered by a new player on PC, with only some options being available (as of 1.14).
The main menu is one of the several menus in Vampire Survivors. Once the player loads the game, a flashing "PRESS TO START" prompt will appear. When clicked, the player will gain access to the main menu.
This menu showcases a variety of options that are available from the start of the game, such as the unlocks and collection menus. However, some options, such as the Bestiary and the Secrets menus, will only become visible by doing certain tasks. In many cases, these tasks would be to obtain certain relics, such as the  Ars Gouda and the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane, respectively, but for Quick Start, the player has to unlock all of the five main stages.
## Options
The main menu encountered by a mobile player who has obtained all of the relics needed to unlock the rest of the options.
The following table lists all of the main menu options below the top navigation bar, from top to bottom, left to right, then lists the options in the top navigation bar.
| Option | Description | Unlocking
| DLC Store (mobile only) | Accesses the DLC Store, which lists all of the expansions. | ? (edit)
| Start | Accesses the character selection menu. | Available by default.
| Quick Start | Immediately accesses a random stage with a randomly selected character. | Unlocking the five main stages of the game: Mad Forest, Inlaid Library, Dairy Plant, Gallo Tower, and Cappella Magna.
| Online | Accesses online mode. | Available by default on all platforms, excluding iOS and Android devices. However, players on mobile devices can access this mode, as long as they have bought at least one paid DLC and have enabled landscape mode in their options.
| Adventures | Accesses the adventure selection menu. | Obtaining the Atlas Gate from Boss Rash.
| Credits | Lists everyone who has contributed to the development and growth of the game. | Available by default.
| Bestiary | Lists all of the enemies encountered by the player so far. The bestiary entries include a description, stats, and other information about each enemy in the game. | Obtaining the Ars Gouda from Dairy Plant.
| Collection | Lists all of the items encountered by the player so far. The types of items listed in the collection include weapons, passives, pickups, relics, and arcanas. | Available by default.
| Power Up | A shop where players can purchase various PowerUps in the game. These PowerUps grant permanent stat improvements to all characters. | Available by default.
| Unlocks | A menu that lists various tasks that players can accomplish to unlock new items, characters, and more. | Available by default.
| Secrets | A menu that allows players to type in hints to instantly unlock items, characters, and more. | Obtaining the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane from The Bone Zone.
## Top navigation
| Option | Description
| Poncle Account | Enables the player to create or log in to a Poncle Account to use cross-platform saves.
| Quit | Immediately exits the game.
| Options | Accesses the options menu.
## Trivia
- The track that briefly plays upon accessing the main menu is an unlisted track titled "Side A."
- However, if the player has enabled the "Blast Processed" option, the Side B track will be played instead.
- The only green buttons are the "ADVENTURES", "POWER UP", and "DLC STORE" buttons.
- The "POWER UP" button is the only green button that is available from the start of the game.
- The only two buttons in the main menu that have a lower height and are in lowercase are the "quick start" and the "credits" buttons.
- The "credits" button is the only squished and lowercase button that is available from the start of the game.

## Plushes
 - 
 [Official plush image of Mask of the Red Death from Youtooz.]
 Official plush image of Mask of the Red Death from Youtooz.
In August 2022, Slothie, an employee from poncle, asked the community in the official Vampire Survivors Discord server that what character, weapon, enemy, or other item in the game, should receive a plush. The community could respond using Discord reactions.  Mask of the Red Death has received the most reactions, so that made them the character that was chosen to be made into a plush.
2 years later, on 27 September 2024, the plush became available to purchase on Youtooz.

This article is about the DLC. For other uses, see Ode to Castlevania (disambiguation).
| Ode to Castlevania
| 
| Expansion
| Release date | 31 October 2024 (Update)
| Platform(s) | PC, Xbox, PlayStation, Nintendo Switch, iOS, Android
| Retail price | $3.99
| Theme | Castlevania
| {"image":"Ode to Castlevania.jpg","name":"Ode to Castlevania","theme":"Castlevania","release":"31 October 2024","price":"$3.99","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","platform":"PC, Xbox, PlayStation, Nintendo Switch, iOS, Android"}
The sun has set on Dracula's Castle once more, and as the moon rises a hellish horde of murky Mudmen, ghastly ghosts, and frightening Fleamen take the stage as the battle of good and evil rages once again. Unleash an arsenal of new Vampire-vanquishing weapons upon these beasts and leave a trail of doom in your wake before attempting to rid the world of the unspeakable terror himself. Do not be blind to the shadow that looms over you Survivors, as your quest has only just begun! It's all very serious stuff, so now let's pump up the volume and let's go, this is Castlevania in Vampire Survivors, baby!
Official description
Ode to Castlevania is the fifth expansion for Vampire Survivors, released on October 31, 2024. Released with 80 new characters and 107 new weapons, it is the largest expansion of the game to date. It is themed around the Konami gothic action horror franchise Castlevania.
On 28 October 2025, nearly a year later, this expansion received more content.
## Features
## Original release
- 80 new characters - Find Dracula in his throne room with Richter Belmont. Gives 20 new characters. Then explore the new areas in Ode of Castlevania to get the other 40. Have fun unlocking them all!
- 107 new weapons
- Special Weapon Selectors
- A monstrously large new stage
- 91 music tracks*
- A ridiculous amount of remixes of Castlevania tracks from Vampire Survivors historic composers Daniele Zandara and Filippo Vicarelli, but also from fan-favorites Evelyn Lark and Keygen Church!
- ♫ Vampire Killer, Bloody Tears, Simon's Theme, Leon's Theme, a lot of themes.
- ♫ Moonlight Nocturne, Lost Paintings, Dance of Pearls, but like you've never heard them before.
- ♫ Aquarius, The Beginning, Divine Bloodlines, everything that screams "this is Castlevania".
- ♫ An Empty Tome, Invitation of a Crazed Moon, Pitch Black Intrusion, so many hits from the more recent entries in the series.
- ♫ Amber Scenery, Sarabande of Healing, Jaws of a Scorched Earth, and many others you didn't know the title of!
- On top of that we have borrowed several dozens original tracks from Castlevania games. There is so much music we revamped the music selection panel for the occasion.
* = poncle mentioned that there are 91 tracks, but there are actually 94 tracks, with three of them being unlisted, which are Mysterious Curse, Pressure and Stage Clear jingle.

# On Revival
On Revival or On Revive is a mechanic in Vampire Survivors where a specific character or item triggers a special skill or mechanic immediately after being revived.
On Revival skills include:
## Characters
-  Dolores triggering a Showstopper.
-  Velvet Dodecahedron triggering any On Revival skills upon leveling up.
-  Malice Bloodmoon triggering a Blood Hex.
-  Congregation spawning a  Velvet Dodecahedron.
-  Sargon summoning a clone.
-  Nameless Fool obtaining up to a total of 3 extra Darkanas when its LastBreath ability is triggered.
## Survarot
- Krochi Freetto Survarot triggering a  Rapid Fire.

Assemble a squad of classic Characters and arm yourself with an explosive arsenal of arcade weaponry in this action packed expansion for Vampire Survivors. Blast through thousands of fearsome foes and bonkers bosses from throughout the lauded lineage of Contra games. Official description
Operation Guns is the fourth expansion for Vampire Survivors, released on May 9, 2024. It is themed after the run and gun video game series Contra, developed by Konami.
## Features
- 12 Contra-Tough characters:
-  Bill Rizer- a big guy with a big gun, bent on vengeance against an alien menace.
Loves: action movies, high-calibre rounds. Hates: Cryogenic coffins, space creatures.
-  Lance Bean- known for attacking aggressively, always having hair dye on hand, and having a brain almost as impressive as his muscles, Lance is partially responsible for creating Contra Force's strange new weapons.
-  Ariana- forced to defend her home amongst the jungles of Galuga from the vile traitors of Red Falcon, Ariana is an expert in evasive manoeuvres, turning her Movement Speed into extra Armour.
-  Lucia Zero- is she a clone? A cyborg? A tabula-rasa terror tutored by Team Contra to be the perfect warrior? Her past is a mystery, but her deadliness on the battlefield is well known, as is her reputation for rebounding from any supposed defeat even stronger than ever.
-  Brad Fang- the bad-butt product of twisted genetics and cutting edge cyber-tech, Brad Fang is half-man, half-wolf, half-robot, and 150% super soldier.
-  Browny- designed to be an all-purpose battle bot, a string of strange bugs (or features) in Browny's programming have caused this diminutive droid to be obsessed with partying. Fortunately, this calamitous coding error does nothing to impede Browny's prodigious combat prowess.
-  Sheena Etranzi- a guerrilla combat genius especially skilled in jungle combat and all manner of experimental weaponry, Sheena is a true survivor and no stranger to prevailing against impossible odds.
-  Probotector- a hyper advanced recon robot programmed to protect humans in inimical environments, this RD009 Probotector Model can convert any enhancements to its Armour into more Might for its many modular weapons.
-  Stanley

# Options
Settings
This article is about the in-game options. For the Vampire Survivors Wiki's account settings, see Special:Preferences.
Options is an interface accessed from either the main menu or pause menu. There are six sections of options: Favorites, Display, Sound, Gameplay, Co-op, and Account.
It also contains a list of currently-installed DLC versions and the base game version.
Some of the game options can also be accessed through the pause menu. # Overhealing
Overhealing is an event in Vampire Survivors. It happens whenever a character gets healed over their Max Health. Certain effects will only trigger upon overhealing.
## Characters
Overhealing will trigger the following effects (effectiveness depends on the amount of overhealed health):
-  Gazebo gains a cumulative bonus to a random stat every time they overheal by +30.
-  Miang Moonspell [DLC] gains +0.2 Max Health each time she overheals.
-  Menya Moonspell [DLC] triggers the weapon in her first slot when overhealing by +16.
-  Syuuto Moonspell [DLC] gains +3% Might when overhealing by +16.
-  Babi-Onna [DLC] gains +30% Move Speed for 10 seconds when overhealing by +16.
-  Megalo Miang Moonspell [DLC] gains +0.2 Max Health each time she overheals.
- Additionally, by nature of her passive, all overhealing is converted to Max Health.
-  Megalo Menya Moonspell [DLC] triggers the weapon in her first slot when overhealing by +16.
-  Megalo Syuuto Moonspell [DLC] increases the Base Damage of  Blood damage weapons when he overheals by +16.
-  McCoy-Oni [DLC] gains +1 Reroll when overhealing by +16.
-  Spiritoso [DLC] summons  Mille Bolle Blu [DLC],  LunarMight [DLC],  LunarBight [DLC], or  LunarSight [DLC] when overhealing by +64.
-  Spiritosa [DLC] summons a follower when overhealing by +16.
-  Sonia [DLC] gains temporary Cooldown reduction.
-  Grant [DLC] triggers all knife weapons equipped when being overhealed by +8.
-  Alucard [DLC] triggers  Summon Spirit when being overhealed by +8.
-  Maxim [DLC] gains Speed based on total overhealing.
-  Vincent Dorin [DLC] gains Greed based on total overhealing.
-  Maria [DLC] gains Might based on total overhealing. Overhealing by +32 temporary buffs all her Followers.
-  Barlowe [DLC] gains Might and Curse based on total overhealing.
-  Wind [DLC] triggers  Aura Blast and a random equipped  Coat of Arms weapon when being overhealed by +8.
-  Walter [DLC] triggers a random equipped  Ebony Diabologue weapon when being overhealed by +8.
-  Keremet [DLC] triggers  Keremet Morbus when being overhealed by +8.
-  Drolta Tzuentes [DLC] triggers all  Belnades' Spellbook weapons when being overhealed by +32.
-  Atlantis Shrine Wizard [DLC] triggers all  Coat of Arms weapons and slowly floods the environment when being overhealed by +32.
-  Guardian's Targe [DLC] triggers extra projectiles.
-  Sacred Beasts Tower Shield [DLC] triggers extra projectiles and provides temporary invulnerability.
## Survarots
-  Suor Clerici: Overhealing by +16 triggers a  Sorbetto.
- Skill: Overhealing by +16 increases  Gold Finger and  Gold Fever duration by +1%. (Grants +1%  Fever)
- Skill: Overhealing by +16 triggers a  Sorbetto.
- Skill: Overhealing by +16 grants +1  Reroll.
- Skill: Overhealing by +16 grants +3%  Might.
Note that overhealing-based skills worded as "+X" require that much Health to be overhealed in a single, or incredibly rapid, instance(s), rather than the skills occuring based on total overhealing.
- For example, Gazebo will not gain stats every 30 Health overhealed. Rather, 30 Health must be overhealed at once.

# Player stats
This article or section contains information that is out of date.
Reason: Missing OtC, ED, BM DLCs. Modified by should be cleaned up and/or automated.
You can help improve this page by editing it.
An example of a list of player stats.
Stats (short for statistics) are numerical attributes that a player has in Vampire Survivors, which affect the player's character and how the environment reacts to them. They can be seen before the player starts the game in the Character Selection menu, in-game when paused, or when leveling up.
All stats can be modified by PowerUps, and in addition most of them have their own passive item that provides a bonus. Also most characters have some stats with modifications to the respective base value (e.g. 100 Max Health and 30 Magnet).

There are 26 different stats in the game, all of which directly apply to the player's character, with the exception of Seal.

Poncle (stylised in all lowercase letters as poncle) is the development team behind Vampire Survivors. It was founded by Luca Galante and was named after his pseudonym. It is currently headquartered in London, the United Kingdom, though they are going to have a branch in Japan, per the update post for the v1.15 update.
